AND the turn-timing for ray cells (as above: don't END ≡2 on a ray; don't ENTER a ray during ≡0).    Sub-goal priority: (1) if keys missing and their cell is KNOWN-REACHABLE → target it; (2) else nearest frontier (with a tiebreak toward the missing keys); (3) if both keys held → target V similarly; (4) if nothing →... camp safe.
